| 62b7639a5a68:20 | How Germany taxes an individual who is fully liable to German income tax and holds crypto assets (Kryptowerte) privately, not in a business. It covers selling and swapping (private sales under § 23 EStG), which units count as sold, staking and lending income (§ 22 Nr. 3 EStG), mining and forging, airdrops, hard forks, NFTs, the records the tax office expects, and the new platform reporting (DAC8 / CARF) that starts with 2026. | Exact wording retained; current LF20, Crypto tax in Germany |
| 62b7639a5a68:22 | **Primary year: 2026 (calendar year).** A dated section covers the 2025 return being filed now. The statute figures come from the consolidated federal law on gesetze-im-internet.de. The rules on how to apply them come from the finance ministry's letter of 6 March 2025, "Einzelfragen zur ertragsteuerrechtlichen Behandlung bestimmter Kryptowerte" (GZ IV C 1 - S 2256/00042/064/043), which replaced the letter of 10 May 2022. Paragraph numbers (Rn) below all point into that letter: [BMF letter of 6 March 2025](https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Steuerarten/Einkommensteuer/2025-03-06-einzelfragen-kryptowerte-bmf-schreiben.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=2). Do not use the paragraph numbers of the 2022 letter. | Exact wording retained; current LF22, Crypto tax in Germany |
| 62b7639a5a68:24 | **Not covered:** business holdings, wages in crypto, non-residents, treaties, VAT, gift and inheritance tax. | Exact wording retained; current LF24, Crypto tax in Germany |
| 62b7639a5a68:26 | **How the letter sorts tokens (Rn 2 to 4)**, by function whatever the name: currency or payment tokens (means of exchange or speculation; the letter names Bitcoin and Ether), utility tokens (a right to use a network or obtain a product), security tokens (work like securities), and hybrids. A utility token used as a means of exchange is treated like a currency token when used that way. | Exact wording retained; current LF26, Crypto tax in Germany |
| 62b7639a5a68:30 | - Held privately, or in a business or company? Any wages paid in crypto? Fully liable to German tax all year? | Exact wording retained; current LF30, Ask the client first |
| 62b7639a5a68:31 | - Which wallets and platforms (any foreign or decentralised)? Complete exports for every year you held coins? | Exact wording retained; current LF31, Ask the client first |
| 62b7639a5a68:32 | - For each sale, swap or crypto payment: coin, units, wallet, date bought, date sold, and for what? Can you show which units were sold, and which lot method did you use before for that coin in that wallet? | Exact wording retained; current LF32, Ask the client first |
| 62b7639a5a68:33 | - Any staking, lending, mining, forging, airdrops or forks? For an airdrop: did you have to post, upload or hand over personal data? | Exact wording retained; current LF33, Ask the client first |
| 62b7639a5a68:34 | - Did you stake or lend a token that is NOT a currency token? | Exact wording retained; current LF34, Ask the client first |
| 62b7639a5a68:35 | - Any NFTs, derivatives, leveraged trading, liquidity pools or security tokens? | Exact wording retained; current LF35, Ask the client first |
| 62b7639a5a68:36 | - Which tax report tool, price source and settings? | Exact wording retained; current LF36, Ask the client first |
| 62b7639a5a68:37 | - Wages, other private sales in the same year, joint assessment, losses carried forward? Were earlier returns complete? | Exact wording retained; current LF37, Ask the client first |
| 62b7639a5a68:41 | 1. **Sort each asset** by function (Rn 2 to 4). Security tokens, NFTs and anything held in a business leave this skill. | Exact wording retained; current LF41, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:42 | 2. **Sort each event.** Sale, swap or paying with crypto: private sale, § 23 EStG (Rn 54). Passive staking and lending: § 22 Nr. 3 EStG (Rn 48, 65). Mining or forging: test [§ 15(2) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__15.html) first (Rn 35 to 39). Moving coins between own wallets: no sale (Rn 54). | Exact wording retained; current LF42, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:43 | 3. **Fix the dates.** Central platform: the times it recorded. Direct trades, for example on a decentralised platform: as a rule the wallet times. A client who wants the contract date to count must prove it (Rn 55). | Exact wording retained; current LF43, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:44 | 4. **Pick the units sold** under Rn 61 and 62 (below), per wallet and per coin, and write the method down. | Exact wording retained; current LF44, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:45 | 5. **Test the holding period.** A sale is taxable only if the time between purchase and sale is "nicht mehr als ein Jahr", not more than one year ([§ 23(1) sentence 1 no. 2 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__23.html)). Each swap starts a new period for the coin received (Rn 55). | Exact wording retained; current LF45, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:46 | 6. **Compute each taxable gain or loss:** sale price less purchase cost less costs of the sale (§ 23(3) sentence 1 EStG; Rn 57 to 60). | Exact wording retained; current LF46, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:47 | 7. **Add up all private sales of the year per person**, crypto and non-crypto together, and test the total against the Freigrenze. A total below the limit is tax-free; at or above it, the whole total is taxed. | Exact wording retained; current LF47, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:48 | 8. **Compute § 22 Nr. 3 income:** every reward at its euro market price when received, less the related costs, added to all other income from services of the year. Test the result against its own Freigrenze. | Exact wording retained; current LF48, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:49 | 9. **Keep losses in their box:** § 23 losses only against § 23 gains, § 22 Nr. 3 losses only against § 22 Nr. 3 income, one year back and later years forward. | Exact wording retained; current LF49, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:50 | 10. **Report** in Anlage SO of the income tax return through ELSTER (business mining: Anlage G and the profit statement). File on time (Filing and payment below), and keep the records listed in "Records and duties to cooperate". | Exact wording retained; current LF50, The method, step by step |
| 62b7639a5a68:54 | \| Figure \| 2026 \| 2025 \| Source \| | Exact wording retained; current LF54, Figures for 2026 and 2025 |
| 62b7639a5a68:56 | \| Freigrenze for all private sales of the year (a cliff: the total must be BELOW it) \| EUR 1,000 \| EUR 1,000 \| [§ 23(3) sentence 5 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__23.html): "weniger als 1 000 Euro"; Rn 53 \| | Exact wording retained; current LF56, Figures for 2026 and 2025 |
| 62b7639a5a68:57 | \| Same limit for years up to and including 2023 (late or corrected returns only) \| n/a \| n/a \| Rn 53: "bis Veranlagungszeitraum 2023: 600 €" \| | Exact wording retained; current LF57, Figures for 2026 and 2025 |
| 62b7639a5a68:58 | \| Freigrenze for all income from services (§ 22 Nr. 3), tested after costs; a cliff \| EUR 256 \| EUR 256 \| [§ 22 Nr. 3 sentence 2 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__22.html): "weniger als 256 Euro im Kalenderjahr" \| | Exact wording retained; current LF58, Figures for 2026 and 2025 |
| 62b7639a5a68:59 | \| Employees: untaxed side income that forces an assessment if MORE than \| EUR 410 \| EUR 410 \| [§ 46(2) no. 1 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__46.html) \| | Exact wording retained; current LF59, Figures for 2026 and 2025 |
| 62b7639a5a68:60 | \| Employees who are assessed: side income is deducted again if NOT MORE than \| EUR 410 \| EUR 410 \| § 46(3) EStG \| | Exact wording retained; current LF60, Figures for 2026 and 2025 |
| 62b7639a5a68:61 | \| Record retention of six years applies if positive surplus income is MORE than \| EUR 500,000 \| EUR 500,000 \| [§ 147a AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__147a.html); Rn 105 \| | Exact wording retained; current LF61, Figures for 2026 and 2025 |
| 62b7639a5a68:62 | \| The same threshold from 1 January 2027 \| EUR 750,000 from 2027 \| n/a \| § 147a AO footnote; Rn 105 \| | Exact wording retained; current LF62, Figures for 2026 and 2025 |
| 62b7639a5a68:63 | \| Late-filing surcharge per started month (tax less prepayments and withholding) \| 0.25%, at least EUR 25 \| same \| [§ 152(5) AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__152.html) \| | Exact wording retained; current LF63, Figures for 2026 and 2025 |
| 62b7639a5a68:64 | \| Flat tax on investment income, for contrast only: it does NOT apply to currency-token gains \| 25% \| 25% \| [§ 32d(1) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__32d.html) \| | Exact wording retained; current LF64, Figures for 2026 and 2025 |
| 62b7639a5a68:66 | The two Freigrenzen are separate tests. They are never added together, and using one does not use up the other. Taxable crypto gains and income are added to the client's other income and taxed at the personal progressive rate, plus solidarity surcharge and, for members, church tax. The tariff is in [§ 32a EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__32a.html) and in the German income tax skill, `de-einkommensteuer-freelancer`; this skill does not compute a rate. | Exact wording retained; current LF66, Figures for 2026 and 2025 |
| 62b7639a5a68:70 | - **What is taxed.** Crypto held privately is an "anderes Wirtschaftsgut". A gain on a sale is taxable income from a private sale if not more than one year lies between purchase and sale (Rn 53, citing the Federal Fiscal Court judgment of 14 February 2023, IX R 3/22). The intention to make a profit is not tested (Rn 53). | Exact wording retained; current LF70, Private sales: selling, swapping and paying with crypto (§ 23 EStG) |
| 62b7639a5a68:71 | - **A purchase and a sale are both needed (Rn 54).** A purchase is getting the coin from a third party for something in return: for euro, goods, services or other coins, and also coins received from mining or forging, lending, passive staking, and, where it applies, an ICO or an airdrop. A sale is the mirror image: a swap into euro, goods, services or other coins. | Exact wording retained; current LF71, Private sales: selling, swapping and paying with crypto (§ 23 EStG) |
| 62b7639a5a68:72 | - **Swaps.** Coin for coin is a sale of the coin given up and a purchase of the coin received. The sale price is the market price of the coin RECEIVED at the time of the swap; if that cannot be found, the market price of the coin given up is accepted (Rn 58). That market price, plus incidental purchase costs, is also the purchase cost of the coin received (Rn 59). "Die Veräußerungsfristen ... beginnen nach jedem Tausch neu" (Rn 55). | DE-L04 source correction; currentLF72 separately identifies given-asset basis and received-asset disposal proceeds. Original words preserved here. |
| 62b7639a5a68:73 | - **Paying for goods or services.** The sale price is the euro price agreed; if none was stated, the market price of the coin given up (Rn 60). This includes a crypto card that sells coins at the till; the card statement is normally enough as a record (Rn 103). | Exact wording retained; current LF73, Private sales: selling, swapping and paying with crypto (§ 23 EStG) |
| 62b7639a5a68:74 | - **Costs.** Transaction fees on the sale are deductible (Rn 59). Costs must be split between taxable sales and sales that are not taxable, such as sales after more than one year (Rn 57). | Exact wording retained; current LF74, Private sales: selling, swapping and paying with crypto (§ 23 EStG) |
| 62b7639a5a68:75 | - **Change outputs.** For coins on the UTXO model, the "change" that flows back to the client's own key keeps the original purchase data (Rn 56). | Exact wording retained; current LF75, Private sales: selling, swapping and paying with crypto (§ 23 EStG) |
| 62b7639a5a68:76 | - **Gifts.** A gift is not a sale by the giver. The recipient takes over the giver's purchase ([§ 23(1) sentence 3 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__23.html); Rn 75), so the giver's purchase date counts for the one-year test. | Exact wording retained; current LF76, Private sales: selling, swapping and paying with crypto (§ 23 EStG) |
| 62b7639a5a68:77 | - **Market price.** The price on a trading platform or a web price list (Rn 43). A documented daily price is accepted "bis auf Weiteres" if applied evenly: one source and one method for cost and sale price (Rn 91). | Exact wording retained; current LF77, Private sales: selling, swapping and paying with crypto (§ 23 EStG) |
| 62b7639a5a68:81 | This matches de-capital-gains. | Exact wording retained; current LF81, Which units were sold (Rn 61 and 62) |
| 62b7639a5a68:83 | - **Individual identification first (Einzelbetrachtung).** Where the client can show which units were sold, those units count. | Exact wording retained; current LF83, Which units were sold (Rn 61 and 62) |
| 62b7639a5a68:84 | - **If that is not possible:** for the HOLDING PERIOD, the first-bought units of that coin (for example Bitcoin or Ether) count as sold first; for the VALUE, the average-cost method (Durchschnittsmethode) applies. As a simplification, first in, first out may be used for the value as well. | Exact wording retained; current LF84, Which units were sold (Rn 61 and 62) |
| 62b7639a5a68:85 | - **Per wallet:** "Es gilt eine walletbezogene Betrachtung." There is no single queue across all wallets. | Exact wording retained; current LF85, Which units were sold (Rn 61 and 62) |
| 62b7639a5a68:86 | - **Per coin:** each coin name in a wallet carries its own choice. | Exact wording retained; current LF86, Which units were sold (Rn 61 and 62) |
| 62b7639a5a68:87 | - **Keep it.** Inside a wallet, the chosen method stays until every unit of that coin in that wallet has been sold. After a full sale and a new purchase, the method may be changed. | Exact wording retained; current LF87, Which units were sold (Rn 61 and 62) |
| 62b7639a5a68:88 | - **Document it:** the method per wallet and coin, and reallocations within wallets (Rn 103). | Exact wording retained; current LF88, Which units were sold (Rn 61 and 62) |
| 62b7639a5a68:89 | - LIFO and "highest in, first out" are not among the methods the letter offers. | Exact wording retained; current LF89, Which units were sold (Rn 61 and 62) |
| 62b7639a5a68:93 | - Losses from private sales offset only gains from private sales of the same year, up to those gains. They cannot be set against wages, business income, investment income or § 22 Nr. 3 income ([§ 23(3) sentence 7 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__23.html)). | Exact wording retained; current LF93, Losses from private sales |
| 62b7639a5a68:94 | - What is left reduces private-sales gains of the year before or of later years, under the rules of § 10d EStG; the remaining loss is formally determined (§ 23(3) sentence 8 EStG). On application the carry-back is waived altogether; the law allows no partial waiver ("insgesamt abzusehen", [§ 10d(1) sentence 6 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__10d.html)). Declare losses even in a year with no tax, so the loss is recorded. | Exact wording retained; current LF94, Losses from private sales |
| 62b7639a5a68:95 | - Is the Freigrenze tested before or after losses carried in from other years? The law tests the "Gesamtgewinn" of the year; how carried-in losses interact with the limit is a "check" point for a Steuerberater. | Exact wording retained; current LF95, Losses from private sales |
| 62b7639a5a68:101 | - **Passive staking** (a staking pool or platform staking, without creating blocks oneself) is "in der Regel" income from services under § 22 Nr. 3 EStG (Rn 48). Running a masternode or creating blocks oneself (forging) follows the mining rules below (Rn 50). | Exact wording retained; current LF101, Staking and lending (§ 22 Nr. 3 EStG) |
| 62b7639a5a68:102 | - **Lending** income is § 22 Nr. 3 income (Rn 65): coins are let out for a time for a fee. | Exact wording retained; current LF102, Staking and lending (§ 22 Nr. 3 EStG) |
| 62b7639a5a68:103 | - **Value.** Each reward is taken at its euro market price when received (Rn 48, 65), or at a consistent daily price (Rn 91). Simplification: during the year the time the reward is booked into the wallet on claiming may be used; rewards not yet claimed count at the latest at the end of the calendar year (Rn 48a). | Exact wording retained; current LF103, Staking and lending (§ 22 Nr. 3 EStG) |
| 62b7639a5a68:104 | - **Freigrenze.** Income from services is untaxed if it is "weniger als 256 Euro im Kalenderjahr" (§ 22 Nr. 3 sentence 2 EStG). It covers ALL § 22 Nr. 3 income of the person together: staking, lending, private mining, airdrops received for a service, and non-crypto income such as occasional commissions. It is tested on income after costs (receipts less Werbungskosten), and at or above the limit the whole amount is taxed. | Exact wording retained; current LF104, Staking and lending (§ 22 Nr. 3 EStG) |
| 62b7639a5a68:105 | - **Losses** from services cannot be set against other income; they reduce § 22 Nr. 3 income of the year before or of later years under § 10d EStG (§ 22 Nr. 3 sentences 3 and 4). | Exact wording retained; current LF105, Staking and lending (§ 22 Nr. 3 EStG) |
| 62b7639a5a68:106 | - **The rewards themselves.** Each reward counts as purchased (Rn 54, 65), so each starts its own one-year period, and a later sale within that period is a private sale. | Exact wording retained; current LF106, Staking and lending (§ 22 Nr. 3 EStG) |
| 62b7639a5a68:107 | - **The staked or lent coins.** For currency tokens the one-year period is NOT extended to ten years (Rn 63). The statute extends the period to ten years for other assets "aus deren Nutzung als Einkunftsquelle zumindest in einem Kalenderjahr Einkünfte erzielt werden" ([§ 23(1) sentence 1 no. 2 sentence 4 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__23.html)). The letter excludes only currency tokens from that rule, so a staked or lent utility token or other non-currency token can fall under the ten-year period. Refer. | Exact wording retained; current LF107, Staking and lending (§ 22 Nr. 3 EStG) |
| 62b7639a5a68:108 | - **Lent coins coming back.** The letter does not say whether the return of lent coins is a sale (Rn 26, 65). "Check". | Exact wording retained; current LF108, Staking and lending (§ 22 Nr. 3 EStG) |
| 62b7639a5a68:112 | - **Mining and forging are purchases**: block reward and transaction fees are both received in exchange for a service (Rn 33, 34, 42). | Exact wording retained; current LF112, Mining and forging (block creation) |
| 62b7639a5a68:113 | - **Business or not.** Block creation is a business under [§ 15(2) EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__15.html) when independent, lasting (set up to be repeated, Rn 36), able to make a profit over time (Rn 37) and taking part in general trade, which offering computing power to the network already is (Rn 38). It is not private asset management (Rn 39). A mining pool can be a partnership depending on the contract; a staking pool is as a rule not one (Rn 40). | Exact wording retained; current LF113, Mining and forging (block creation) |
| 62b7639a5a68:114 | - **As a business:** coins received are valued at market price when received (Rn 43). With a cash-basis profit statement the receipt is business income, and the coins' cost is deducted only when they are sold or withdrawn (Rn 44). Trade tax and bookkeeping follow; refer to a business skill. | Exact wording retained; current LF114, Mining and forging (block creation) |
| 62b7639a5a68:115 | - **Not a business**, for example because it is not lasting: § 22 Nr. 3 income ([Rn 45](https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Steuerarten/Einkommensteuer/2025-03-06-einzelfragen-kryptowerte-bmf-schreiben.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=2)), valued at market price when received. Hardware and software (by depreciation where needed) and electricity are deductible costs (Rn 47). The EUR 256 limit above applies together with all other income from services (Rn 45). | Exact wording retained; current LF115, Mining and forging (block creation) |
| 62b7639a5a68:119 | - **Income only for a service.** An airdrop is § 22 Nr. 3 income where the client has to do something for it: for example naming the airdrop or the project in social media posts, uploading own pictures or videos (Rn 70), or handing over personal data beyond what the technical transfer needs; the public key alone is enough for a transfer (Rn 71). | Exact wording retained; current LF119, Airdrops (Rn 70 to 75) |
| 62b7639a5a68:120 | - **Chance.** Where chance also decides who receives, the link between service and reward is broken or overlaid (Rn 72). | Exact wording retained; current LF120, Airdrops (Rn 70 to 75) |
| 62b7639a5a68:121 | - **No service at all:** a gift may be possible, and the gift tax rules must be considered (Rn 74). Refer. | Exact wording retained; current LF121, Airdrops (Rn 70 to 75) |
| 62b7639a5a68:122 | - **Value:** market price when received; if no market price can be found yet, a value of zero is accepted (Rn 73). | Exact wording retained; current LF122, Airdrops (Rn 70 to 75) |
| 62b7639a5a68:123 | - **Cost for a later sale.** An airdrop received for a service is also a purchase. Its cost is the value of the data or action given, presumed (rebuttably) to equal the market price of the tokens (Rn 75). | Exact wording retained; current LF123, Airdrops (Rn 70 to 75) |
| 62b7639a5a68:127 | - A hard fork does NOT create § 22 Nr. 3 income (Rn 68). | Exact wording retained; current LF127, Hard forks (Rn 67 and 68) |
| 62b7639a5a68:128 | - Buying coins always includes a purchase of the coins a later fork creates. The cost of the original coins is split between old and new coins in the ratio of their market prices at the time of the fork. If the new coins have no value, all the cost stays with the old coins (Rn 67). | Exact wording retained; current LF128, Hard forks (Rn 67 and 68) |
| 62b7639a5a68:129 | - The new coins take the purchase date of the original coins. A sale of the new coins is a private sale only if not more than one year lies between the purchase of the ORIGINAL coins and the sale (Rn 68). New coins from long-held originals can therefore be sold outside § 23 at once. | Exact wording retained; current LF129, Hard forks (Rn 67 and 68) |
| 62b7639a5a68:133 | - **Utility tokens.** Redeeming a token for the product or service it stands for is not a sale (Rn 79). Selling a purchased utility token can be a private sale, also where it is used as a means of exchange (Rn 80). | Exact wording retained; current LF133, Utility tokens, security tokens and ICOs |
| 62b7639a5a68:134 | - **Security tokens** can be securities or other financial instruments; current income and gains can then be investment income under § 20 EStG at the flat rate, depending on the token's terms (Rn 81 to 86). Refer; see de-capital-gains. | Exact wording retained; current LF134, Utility tokens, security tokens and ICOs |
| 62b7639a5a68:135 | - **ICO.** Tokens bought in an ICO for euro or other coins are purchased (Rn 54); cost is what was given (Rn 59). The issuer's side is outside this skill (Rn 76). | Exact wording retained; current LF135, Utility tokens, security tokens and ICOs |
| 62b7639a5a68:139 | The letter says: "Auf die Besonderheiten nicht-fungibler Kryptowerte (sog. Non-Fungible-Token, NFT) wird in der vorliegenden Fassung des BMF-Schreibens nicht eingegangen" (Rn 5). The ministry's notice page for the letter also says NFTs and liquidity mining are not yet covered and that the letter will be added to over time. The law is therefore unsettled: the private-sales rule for "other assets" in § 23 EStG is the obvious starting point for an NFT bought and sold privately, but the ministry has not confirmed it, and income from creating or licensing NFTs may be business or other income. Refer, and check the ministry's site for a newer version of the letter. | Exact wording retained; current LF139, NFTs |
| 62b7639a5a68:143 | Liquidity pools and liquidity mining, yield farming, wrapped tokens, governance rewards, stablecoins as such, derivatives and leveraged trading, and lost, stolen or scammed coins have no rule in the letter. A § 23 loss needs a sale, a transfer to a third party for something in return (Rn 54, 57); the letter mentions hacks only as a records risk that falls on the client (Rn 89). Do not tell a client that a lost key, a hack or a scam gives a deductible loss. Refer all of these. | Exact wording retained; current LF143, Other points the letter does not settle |
| 62b7639a5a68:147 | For crypto held privately the general duties of Rn 87 to 92 apply (Rn 100). | Exact wording retained; current LF147, Records and duties to cooperate (Rn 87 to 105) |
| 62b7639a5a68:149 | - **Truth and cooperation.** Returns must be made truthfully to the best of the client's knowledge ([§ 150(2) AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__150.html)); the client must help establish the facts ([§ 90 AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__90.html)) (Rn 88). | Exact wording retained; current LF149, Records and duties to cooperate (Rn 87 to 105) |
| 62b7639a5a68:150 | - **Blockchain data is the client's burden:** a public key alone is not evidence (Rn 87). | Exact wording retained; current LF150, Records and duties to cooperate (Rn 87 to 105) |
| 62b7639a5a68:151 | - **Extended duty for foreign and decentralised platforms.** Trading on a central platform run from abroad triggers the extended duty of § 90(2) AO: the client must clear up the facts and obtain the evidence, in particular by downloading the platforms' transaction overviews regularly and in full. Missing records and lost data, for example after a platform's insolvency or a hack, count against the client. The same applies as a rule to decentralised platforms (Rn 89). | Exact wording retained; current LF151, Records and duties to cooperate (Rn 87 to 105) |
| 62b7639a5a68:152 | - **Tax reports.** A report from a private provider can be the basis of the assessment if it looks plausible: nothing obviously missing (costs, wallets, platforms), consistent in itself, and not at odds with what the office knows. As a rule the report settings (prices, lot method such as first in, first out) and the tax reasoning must be shown; manual changes must be marked and explained (Rn 90). The office can ask for the files behind the report (Rn 101). | Exact wording retained; current LF152, Records and duties to cooperate (Rn 87 to 105) |
| 62b7639a5a68:153 | - **Minimum per sale.** Records should let each private sale be traced on its own: coin name or ticker, number of units, the gain with cost and sale price (or times and prices of purchase and sale), and the holding period (Rn 102). For lending and other services: start, end, object, fee and terms (Rn 102). | Exact wording retained; current LF153, Records and duties to cooperate (Rn 87 to 105) |
| 62b7639a5a68:154 | - **What the office may ask for (Rn 101, 103, 104):** for each purchase and sale the time, amount, type, platform, euro values and price source; the lot method per wallet and coin and reallocations within wallets; records of reward income (for an airdrop, its conditions); and in a single case the source of funds, wallet balances at 31 December of the year and the year before, wallet addresses, transaction hashes, platform account details, and screenshots once the office has used its own means such as a block explorer. | Exact wording retained; current LF154, Records and duties to cooperate (Rn 87 to 105) |
| 62b7639a5a68:155 | - **Estimation.** Where the office cannot work out the figures it estimates them under [§ 162 AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__162.html). The estimate must aim at the real facts and must not be used as a penalty (Rn 92). | Exact wording retained; current LF155, Records and duties to cooperate (Rn 87 to 105) |
| 62b7639a5a68:156 | - **How long to keep records.** A private investor whose positive surplus income (wages, investment income, letting and § 22 income, which includes private sales) is more than the § 147a AO threshold in the figures table in a calendar year must keep the records on those receipts and costs for six years ([§ 147a AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__147a.html); Rn 105). For spouses assessed jointly, each spouse's own sum counts. Below the threshold no fixed period is printed, but the purchase record is needed whenever a unit is sold, however long ago it was bought (Rn 102), and assessments stay open for four years as a rule, ten where tax was evaded and five where it was reduced through gross negligence ([§ 169(2) AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__169.html)). | Exact wording retained; current LF156, Records and duties to cooperate (Rn 87 to 105) |
| 62b7639a5a68:160 | The enacted Kryptowerte-Steuertransparenz-Gesetz (KStTG) implements DAC8 and the OECD's CARF. | Exact wording retained; current LF160, Platform reporting from 2026 (DAC8 / CARF) |
| 62b7639a5a68:162 | - **From 2026.** Providers' duties to identify and report users apply "erstmals für das Kalenderjahr 2026" ([§ 21 KStTG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ksttg/__21.html)). The reporting period is the calendar year ([§ 10 KStTG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ksttg/__10.html)). | Exact wording retained; current LF162, Platform reporting from 2026 (DAC8 / CARF) |
| 62b7639a5a68:163 | - **When.** Providers report to the Federal Central Tax Office (BZSt) by 31 July for the previous reporting period ([§ 9 KStTG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ksttg/__9.html)), so the first reports for 2026 are due by 31 July 2027. | Exact wording retained; current LF163, Platform reporting from 2026 (DAC8 / CARF) |
| 62b7639a5a68:164 | - **What.** Identity data, and per crypto asset the aggregated amounts, units and number of transactions by type, such as sales for fiat money and swaps ([§ 11 KStTG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ksttg/__11.html)). | Exact wording retained; current LF164, Platform reporting from 2026 (DAC8 / CARF) |
| 62b7639a5a68:165 | - **Customers who do not cooperate.** A provider must remind, then warn, a user who does not supply the requested information. For a customer relationship begun by 31 December 2025, the provider must then block reportable transactions at the latest 90 days, but not before 60 days, after the original request ([§ 8 KStTG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ksttg/__8.html)). | Exact wording retained; current LF165, Platform reporting from 2026 (DAC8 / CARF) |
| 62b7639a5a68:166 | - The client must still declare every taxable sale and reward (Rn 88). | Exact wording retained; current LF166, Platform reporting from 2026 (DAC8 / CARF) |
| 62b7639a5a68:170 | \| Situation \| Treatment \| Source \| | Exact wording retained; current LF170, Boundary and exception table |
| 62b7639a5a68:172 | \| Currency token sold after MORE than one year \| Outside § 23, not taxed \| § 23(1) s. 1 no. 2 EStG; Rn 53 \| | Exact wording retained; current LF172, Boundary and exception table |
| 62b7639a5a68:173 | \| Sold on the same calendar date one year after purchase \| Still "nicht mehr als ein Jahr": taxable. The day after that date is outside \| § 23 EStG; [§ 108(1) AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__108.html) (periods follow the Civil Code) \| | Exact wording retained; current LF173, Boundary and exception table |
| 62b7639a5a68:174 | \| Private-sales total, or § 22 Nr. 3 income after costs, exactly at its limit \| The WHOLE amount is taxed ("weniger als") \| § 23(3) s. 5; § 22 Nr. 3 s. 2 EStG \| | Exact wording retained; current LF174, Boundary and exception table |
| 62b7639a5a68:175 | \| Currency token staked or lent \| No ten-year extension \| Rn 63 \| | Exact wording retained; current LF175, Boundary and exception table |
| 62b7639a5a68:176 | \| Non-currency token staked or lent \| Ten-year period may apply. Refer \| § 23(1) s. 1 no. 2 s. 4 EStG \| | Exact wording retained; current LF176, Boundary and exception table |
| 62b7639a5a68:177 | \| Move between own wallets \| Not a sale; document it \| Rn 54, 62, 104 \| | Exact wording retained; current LF177, Boundary and exception table |
| 62b7639a5a68:178 | \| Swap coin for coin \| Sale and new purchase; new one-year period \| Rn 54, 55, 58, 59 \| | Exact wording retained; current LF178, Boundary and exception table |

| 62b7639a5a68:195 | - Holding period: first in, first out, so the January 2025 unit counts as sold. | 62b7639a5a68:196 | - The client sells the second Bitcoin on 1 August 2026 for EUR 65,000. The unit left for the holding period is the September 2025 one: held not more than one year, so the sale is taxable. | Exact wording retained; current LF196, Worked cases (rules: [BMF letter](https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Steuerarten/Einkommensteuer/2025-03-06-einzelfragen-kryptowerte-bmf-schreiben.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=2), [§ 23 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__23.html)) |
| 62b7639a5a68:197 | - Value with the average method: cost is the average of both units, EUR 50,000, so the gain is EUR 15,000. With the first-in-first-out simplification for value, the cost is EUR 60,000 and the gain is EUR 5,000. The client must use the same method throughout for Bitcoin in Wallet A until it is empty (Rn 62), and document it (Rn 103). | Exact wording retained; current LF197, Worked cases (rules: [BMF letter](https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Steuerarten/Einkommensteuer/2025-03-06-einzelfragen-kryptowerte-bmf-schreiben.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=2), [§ 23 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__23.html)) |
| 62b7639a5a68:198 | - Either gain is at or above the private-sales limit, so the whole gain is taxed at the personal rate. | Exact wording retained; current LF198, Worked cases (rules: [BMF letter](https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Steuerarten/Einkommensteuer/2025-03-06-einzelfragen-kryptowerte-bmf-schreiben.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=2), [§ 23 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__23.html)) |
| 62b7639a5a68:200 | **Case 2: the cliff (2026).** The client's only private sale in 2026 is a coin held eight months, gain EUR 999: below EUR 1,000, tax-free. If the client also sold gold within a year at a gain of EUR 200, the total is EUR 1,199 and the whole EUR 1,199 is taxed, not just the part above the limit ([§ 23(3) sentence 5 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__23.html)). Losses from other private sales of the same year are set off first (§ 23(3) sentence 7). | Exact wording retained; current LF200, Worked cases (rules: [BMF letter](https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Steuerarten/Einkommensteuer/2025-03-06-einzelfragen-kryptowerte-bmf-schreiben.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=2), [§ 23 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__23.html)) |
| 62b7639a5a68:202 | **Case 3: staking rewards and the EUR 256 limit (2026).** Passive staking rewards worth EUR 300 at receipt; platform fees for the staking of EUR 60; no other § 22 Nr. 3 income. Income after costs is EUR 240: below the limit, not taxed. If the fees had been EUR 40, income would be EUR 260 and all EUR 260 is taxed. Each reward still starts its own one-year period for a later sale (Rn 48, 54; [§ 22 Nr. 3 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__22.html)). | Exact wording retained; current LF202, Worked cases (rules: [BMF letter](https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Steuerarten/Einkommensteuer/2025-03-06-einzelfragen-kryptowerte-bmf-schreiben.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=2), [§ 23 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__23.html)) |
| 62b7639a5a68:204 | **Case 4: an airdrop for personal data plus staking (2026).** The client had to give an email address and date of birth to receive airdropped tokens worth EUR 150 at receipt, and also received staking rewards worth EUR 200, with no costs. The airdrop is § 22 Nr. 3 income (Rn 71). Total income from services EUR 350: at or above the limit, all EUR 350 is taxed. The airdropped tokens have a cost of EUR 150 for a later sale (Rn 75). | Exact wording retained; current LF204, Worked cases (rules: [BMF letter](https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Steuerarten/Einkommensteuer/2025-03-06-einzelfragen-kryptowerte-bmf-schreiben.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=2), [§ 23 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__23.html)) |
| 62b7639a5a68:206 | **Case 5: hard fork (2026).** Coins bought 1 February 2026 for EUR 10,000. A hard fork on 1 May 2026 creates new coins. At the fork, the old coins are worth EUR 18,000 and the new coins EUR 2,000, together EUR 20,000. Cost split: old coins EUR 9,000, new coins EUR 1,000 (Rn 67). No income at the fork (Rn 68). The new coins are sold on 1 July 2026 for EUR 2,500: not more than one year after the purchase of the ORIGINAL coins, so a private sale with a gain of EUR 1,500. At or above the limit: taxed. Had the original coins been bought in 2024, the sale would be outside § 23. | Exact wording retained; current LF206, Worked cases (rules: [BMF letter](https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Steuerarten/Einkommensteuer/2025-03-06-einzelfragen-kryptowerte-bmf-schreiben.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=2), [§ 23 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__23.html)) |
| 62b7639a5a68:208 | **Case 6: late 2025 return with crypto gains.** A client filing without an adviser has taxable 2025 crypto gains and has not filed. The deadline was 31 July 2026: file now. A surcharge of 0.25% of the tax less prepayments and withheld tax per started month, at least EUR 25 per month, may be charged; it is compulsory after 14 months from the year end unless the deadline was extended, the tax is zero or negative, or the tax does not exceed the prepayments and withheld tax ([§ 152 AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__152.html)). | Exact wording retained; current LF208, Worked cases (rules: [BMF letter](https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Steuerarten/Einkommensteuer/2025-03-06-einzelfragen-kryptowerte-bmf-schreiben.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=2), [§ 23 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__23.html)) |
| 62b7639a5a68:212 | - **Same rules.** The Freigrenze was EUR 1,000 and the § 22 Nr. 3 limit EUR 256 in 2025 as in 2026 ([§ 23](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__23.html), [§ 22](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__22.html)). | 62b7639a5a68:226 | - Past years with undeclared crypto income. A client who finds before the assessment period ends that a return was wrong or incomplete and that tax was or may be underpaid must report it without delay and correct it ([§ 153(1) AO](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__153.html)). Voluntary disclosure and any penalty question go to a Steuerberater or lawyer. | Exact wording retained; current LF226, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:227 | - No usable records: the office may estimate (§ 162 AO; Rn 92). | Exact wording retained; current LF227, When to refuse or refer |
| 62b7639a5a68:231 | - **Who must file.** A person with no wages must file if total income is above the basic allowance (twice that for spouses assessed jointly) ([§ 56 EStDV](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estdv_1955/__56.html)). A return is also compulsory if a remaining loss deduction was formally determined at the end of the previous year, for example a private-sales loss carried forward (§ 56 sentence 2 EStDV). An employee must be assessed if untaxed side income, such as taxable crypto gains or staking income, is more than the § 46(2) no. 1 EStG amount in the figures table; for an employee who is assessed, such income is deducted again if it is not more than that amount in total, and above it a regulation softens the step up to full taxation (§ 46(3) and (5), [§ 46 EStG](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/estg/__46.html)). | Exact wording retained; current LF231, Filing and payment |
| 62b7639a5a68:232 | - **Where.** Anlage SO of the income tax return, filed through ELSTER: the "Leistungen" block for staking, lending, private mining and airdrops, the private-sales block for sales and swaps. Business mining goes on Anlage G with the profit statement. Follow the labels of the form for the year. | Exact wording retained; current LF232, Filing and payment |
| 62b7639a5a68:233 | - **Gains below the limits and losses.** Declare losses from private sales so they can be carried; whether gains below the limits must be entered is set by the form instructions for each year ("check" on the current form). | Exact wording retained; current LF233, Filing and payment |
| 62b7639a5a68:234 | - **Payment.** The tax on private crypto sales and rewards is set in the income tax assessment notice and paid as that notice says. | Exact wording retained; current LF234, Filing and payment |

## Writer controls and actual evidence limits

Exact fractions/date calculations in supplementary-controls.json recompute all six existing prose cases and the DE-L04 separator. The original2025 letter is used as current administrative guidance on its own stated open-case and transitional terms; no old example is fabricated as2026 statutory text. The2026 ELSTER help URL returned only a seven-line generic shell, not operative2026form guidance. Actual2025help was read for2025form labels and its expressly printed inheritance/person/cliff instructions. Those new corroborations do not silently remove current Guide boundaries. No real crypto history, price benchmark, client return, login or filing was used.

Only selected source clauses were read; no whole1299LF BMF or3046LF ELSTER read claim. Detailed actual-read-quotes.json distinguishes acquired bodies from actual selected operative scopes. Current Sources questions on inherited coins, mixed-year losses/freigrenze, non-currency income-use extension, returned lending units and non-covered events are preserved. The statutory reporting method is quoted; comparative DAC8/CARF implementation history is not fabricated from a provider clause. No Guide or Git state was changed by the ledger writer.
